```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales o de una rama privada antes de compartirla, y merge para integrar una rama que ya está publicada o que otros usan, porque reescribir historial compartido causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的，保证数据完整有序送达；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for your own branch that hasn't been shared yet, such as tidying up before opening a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared with others or you want to keep the record of when branches were joined, since rebasing rewrites commits and can cause trouble for anyone who has based work on them.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接指定就行：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里含这段文字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test math
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts -t "adds numbers"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it's applied to the main data, so a crash or power cut can be recovered by replaying the log. That lets the database stay fast and still never lose a committed change.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我去找上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：按 PR #4127 的 review 意见，把锁粒度改细（缩小持锁范围，能分开的锁就分开），在你的分支 ducter/compaction-scheduler 上提交。

做完的标准：1) 相关测试和 CI 在本地或 CI 上跑过，给出结果；2) 说明每把锁改成了什么粒度、为什么这样改，有没有引入死锁或并发风险；3) 报告里附改动的 diff 摘要。

不要做的事：不要合并到主分支，不要推送到 PR 以外的分支，不要替 review 者关闭评论。PR 的 push 先别做，只在本地分支提交，等用户确认。
  [also used tools] task_find
```

  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2025 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt"><span class="k-none">还没看</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
function render(d) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  list.textContent = '';
  if (!d || !Array.isArray(d.trains)) {
    cnt.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没看</span>';
    sub.textContent = '每天早上更新';
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = '明早会填上第一批班次';
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  var under = d.trains.filter(function (t) { return Number(t.price) < 600; });
  cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  sub.textContent = (d.updated ? '更新于 ' + d.updated : '每天早上更新');
  if (!under.length) {
    var n = document.createElement('div');
    n.className = 'k-empty';
    n.textContent = '今天没有 600 元以下的班次';
    list.appendChild(n);
    return;
  }
  under.forEach(function (t) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = t.seats ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '';
    main.appendChild(s);
    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(v);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) {
    ducter.summary(under.length + ' 班 600 元以下');
  }
}
if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined') {
  ducter.onChange(render);
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function (v) {
    render(v && v.trains ? v : null);
  });
} else {
  render(null);
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":{"updated":"11-24","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12,"from":"每天看周五京沪高铁票价"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4,"from":"每天看周五京沪高铁票价"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null,"from":"每天看周五京沪高铁票价"}]}}

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the two nights we have (Night 2 and Night 3), and it will add each night's numbers as the benchmark reports them.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1796 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <span class="k-sum-label">Latest night</span>
  <span class="k-sum-value" id="sv">Not yet</span>
  <span class="k-sum-sub" id="ss">Nightly hawdb write benchmark, runs at 23:00</span>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Nothing yet. Each night's numbers land here.</div>
</div>
<script>
function fmtWrites(w){ return (w/1000).toFixed(0) + 'k writes/s'; }
function render(){
  var runs = (ducter.get('runs') || []).slice().sort(function(a,b){ return b.night - a.night; });
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  empty.style.display = runs.length ? 'none' : '';
  runs.forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = 'Night ' + r.night;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + r.stalls;
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = fmtWrites(r.writes);
    row.appendChild(main); row.appendChild(sub); row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (runs.length) {
    var last = runs[0];
    document.getElementById('sv').textContent = fmtWrites(last.writes);
    document.getElementById('ss').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · night ' + last.night + ' · stalls ' + last.stalls;
    ducter.summary('Night ' + last.night + ': ' + fmtWrites(last.writes) + ', p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms');
  }
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

### app-make-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，点「记一笔」填日期、花在哪和金额，合计和每天的小计会自动更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 4140 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记一笔后，会按天列在下面</div>
  </div>

  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-card">
      <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="d" placeholder="如 11-24"></div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="n" placeholder="如 午饭、打车、酒店"></div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="a" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
      <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记上</button>
    </div>
  </details>
</main>

<script>
const KEY = 'beijing-trip-expenses';
let items = [];
let openId = null;
let lastDay = '';

function load() {
  const v = ducter.get(KEY);
  items = Array.isArray(v && v.items) ? v.items : [];
}
function save() { ducter.set(KEY, { items: items }); }
function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
}
function fmt(n) {
  return '¥' + Number(n).toLocaleString('zh … al));
  else ducter.summary('还没记');
}

document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const day = document.getElementById('d').value.trim();
  const note = document.getElementById('n').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('a').value);
  if (!day || !note || !(amount > 0)) return;
  items.push({ id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day: day, note: note, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100 });
  lastDay = day;
  save();
  document.getElementById('n').value = '';
  document.getElementById('a').value = '';
  document.getElementById('d').value = lastDay;
  render();
});

document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    items = items.filter(it => it.id !== del.getAttribute('data-del'));
    openId = null;
    save();
    render();
    return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
  if (row) {
    const id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    openId = openId === id ? null : id;
    render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(() => { load(); render(); });
load();
document.getElementById('d').value = lastDay;
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

  const total=document.getElementById("total");

  document.getElementById("sub").textContent="北京出差 · "+xs.length+" 笔";

}

document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){

  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

  function dayStr(off) {

    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  }

  function renderDays() {
    const box = $('days');
    box.textContent = '';
    DAY_LABELS.forEach((label, off) => {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'k-chip';
      b.textContent = label;
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(off === dayOff));
      b.onclick = () => { dayOff = off; renderDays(); };
      box.appendChild(b);
    });
  }

  function save() {
    ducter.set('expenses', items);
    render();
  }

  $('save').onclick = () => {
    const amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!(amount > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    const date = dayStr(dayOff);
    items.push({
      id: Date.now(),
      amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100,
      what: $('what').value.trim(),
      category: cat,
      date: date
    });
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('what').value = '';
    save();
  };

  renderDays();
  renderCats();
  load();
  render();
  ducter.onChange(() => { load(); render(); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

var K='feeds', feeds=[], editId=null;
function pad(n){return String(n).padStart(2,'0');}
function today(){var d=new Date();return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate());}
function nowTime(){var d=new Date();return pad(d.getHours())+':'+pad(d.getMinutes());}
function $(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
function el(tag,cls,text){var e=document.createElemen … h?'今天 '+sum+' ml':'今天还没记');
}
function startEdit(id){
  var f=feeds.filter(function(x){return x.id===id;})[0];
  if(!f)return;
  editId=id;
  $('time').value=f.time;$('ml').value=f.ml;
  $('addtitle').textContent='改这一笔';$('save').textContent='保存';$('del').hidden=false;
  $('addbox').open=true;
}
function resetForm(){
  editId=null;$('time').value='';$('ml').value='';
  $('addtitle').textContent='记一笔';$('save').textContent='记下';$('del').hidden=true;
}
function persist(){ducter.set(K,feeds);render();}
$('save').onclick=function(){
  var time=$('time').value.trim()||nowTime();
  var ml=parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if(!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time)||!(ml>0))return;
  if(editId!=null){
    feeds=feeds.map(function(f){return f.id===editId?{id:f.id,date:f.date,time:time,ml:ml}:f;});
  }else{
    feeds.push({id:Date.now(),date:today(),time:time,ml:ml});
  }
  resetForm();$('addbox').open=false;persist();
};
$('del').onclick=function(){
  feeds=feeds.filter(function(f){return f.id!==editId;});
  resetForm();$('addbox').open=false;persist();
};
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(K)).then(function(v){feeds=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];render();});
}
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"date":"2026-11-24","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"date":"2026-11-24","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Made it: a page with your four PRs. Tap one when it lands and the count goes down.
  [an app card under the message: made "Waiting PRs", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2882 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting on someone</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tap a PR when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Pull requests</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
var DEFAULTS = ducter.get("defaults") || [];

var items = null;

function load() {
  var saved = ducter.get("prs");
  items = Array.isArray(saved) && saved.length ? saved : DEFAULTS.map(function (x) { return Object.assign({}, x); });
}

function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  items.forEach(function (it, i) {
    var row = document.createElement("button");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.setAttribute("type", "button");
    row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", it.done ? "true" : "false");
    row.style.width = "100%";
    row.style.textAlign = "left";
    row.style.background = "transparent";
    row.style.border = "0";
    row.style.color = "var(--d-ink)";
    row.style.cursor = "pointer";

    var box = document.createElement("span");
    box.textContent = it.done ? "☑" : "☐";
    box. … ";
    t.textContent = it.title;
    var s = document.createElement("span");
    s.className = "k-row-sub";
    s.style.display = "block";
    s.textContent = it.sub;
    main.appendChild(t);
    main.appendChild(s);

    var val = document.createElement("span");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.style.float = "right";
    val.style.color = it.done ? "var(--d-good)" : "var(--d-soft)";
    val.textContent = it.done ? "landed" : "waiting";

    row.appendChild(box);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    row.addEventListener("click", function () {
      items[i].done = !items[i].done;
      ducter.set("prs", items);
      render();
    });
    list.appendChild(row);
  });

  var waiting = items.filter(function (x) { return !x.done; }).length;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = String(waiting);
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = waiting === 0
    ? "All landed"
    : (items.length - waiting) + " of " + items.length + " landed";
  ducter.summary(waiting + " waiting");
}

load();
render();
ducter.onChange(function () {
  var fresh = ducter.get("prs");
  if (Array.isArray(fresh) && fresh.length) items = fresh;
  render();
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"defaults":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412","sub":"Compaction cut · waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","sub":"Retry on 502 · CI failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","sub":"Flaky compaction test · waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418","sub":"Apps bar · draft","done":false}]}

(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让 TTL 那个任务再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻，补好了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的假时钟基础上，再加一条测试，把时间正好推进到 TTL 到期的那个时刻，检查边界行为：到期那一刻的值该是什么状态（已过期或仍有效），要和代码里现有的 TTL 语义保持一致。如果代码语义不清楚，在报告里写明你选的是哪种约定、为什么。

完成标准：
- 新测试通过，并且确实卡在到期那一刻（前一毫秒有效，到期那一刻按约定处理）。
- 原有的 TTL 测试仍然通过；把新测试连跑几次，把结果贴在报告里。
- 报告里给出测试命令和输出。

不要做的事：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。在自己的分支上提交即可。

地点：~/dev/foo 仓库，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
